> 2026-07-15 **v7**（双身份外审后改写）→ **已发送**

> - **Sent:** 2026-07-15 via Gmail MCP → `sales@coresupplygroup.com.au` · messageId `19f66349d52b9421`
> - 等待：回复 OPEN / 拒绝；若 OPEN → 准备 one-pager + landed-cost sketch（需确认叔叔样品/破损口径）

**Subject:** Core Supply — second kamado line below The Bastard? (21" ~USD $255–316 FOB indicative)

Hi Garrick,

You already put The Bastard VX into Australian retail — I'm not asking you to touch that line.

I'm Kit Lee, export for YUEFU BBQ in Foshan, China (kamados 13"–24"). I'm opening Australia for them. The idea: a house/entry brand *below* The Bastard for margin — not a replacement. First-run supply can stay exclusive through Core Supply if that matters for your retailers.

Indicative only (not locked): mainstream 21" roughly **USD $255–316 FOB**, firm against your spec and MOQ. I can send a 1-pager this week with an AU landed-cost sketch so the margin vs Bastard-tier retail is visible.

Up front on the usual deal-killers: export packing, how breakage claims are handled, and spare-parts lead times — happy to put those in writing, not brochure claims. Low-risk path: **1–2 samples**, then a **small trial qty**, before any container talk.

If that conversation isn't open (exclusivity / bandwidth / timing), say so and I'll stop. If it is, reply **OPEN** and I'll send the one-pager.

Best,
Kit Lee
Export — YUEFU BBQ, Foshan, China

### #369 Atom Grills — 回复 Luke（逐条答 11 问 · 已发）

> 2026-09-21 对齐 · **已发 2026-09-21 22:40 CST** msg `1a0c468c816b0abf` → `Luke@atomgrills.co.uk` cc `sales@atomgrills.co.uk`（thread `1a0bd3b825ef0085`）· 附 `18-inch-kamado-EN1860-1.pdf` + `LFGB-304-Cooking-Grid.pdf` · 厂表 FOB **盐田 USD** · 样品=大货同款整机 · **样品：厂可帮寄、运费据实核算** · **小单：客户自己找货代，单台运费偏高、可谈** · 整柜：货代自提/送到货代仓 · 保修：**正常使用 5 年** · 21"证书/柜下试单 **后补** · 报价口径：**opening numbers，让价挂钩整柜量**（不请对方先报目标价）· 不附中文xlsx · 不点名 Lidl/Aldi · **纯文本发送、不用列表格**（Gmail 纯文本非等宽，`assets/atom-369-reply.html` 仅存档）
>
> **2026-09-21 定稿口径：** ① 数字以**厂 9/21 报价表为准**（18" 252/40HQ · 21" 箱 69×64×90 · GW ~85）——与 2025 目录/已发 one-pager 的 240 / 82kg 不一致，**买家问起再解释，不主动提**；② 18"/21" 标配**含铸铁顶风门 + 温度计**（用户确认）；③ **不发目录、不重发 one-pager**，只附两张证书，信里用 CATALOGUE 段说明并留再联系钩子；④ 首封 $255–316 与本次 $186 的落差**主动说破**，定位成市场价 vs 工厂价；⑤ **本封不提 21" 证书**（不说"明天给"、不说只有 18"），厂 9/22 发来后**另发一封补充**，当作主动加分而非兑现承诺。

**To:** Luke@atomgrills.co.uk  
**Cc:** sales@atomgrills.co.uk  
**Subject:** Re: Complementary ceramic kamado for Atom garden-centre trade?  
**Attachments:** `18-inch-kamado-EN1860-1.pdf` · `LFGB-304-Cooking-Grid.pdf`（**不发目录、不重发 one-pager**）

Hi Luke,

Thanks — the 21" is the right instinct. It is our strongest retail size, and it is the one with no UK home at the moment.

A word first on who you would be buying from, because it changes how you read the rest. YUEFU is our own plant in Foshan: 15,000 m2, 100+ staff, our own tunnel kiln and automatic glaze line, around 60,000 kamados a year. Firing and glazing under one roof is what keeps colour consistent from batch to batch, which is the thing that usually bites a retailer two containers in. We already build private-label kamados for European grocery retail groups, so the QC and packaging discipline an own-brand programme needs is already running here. What we do not have is a UK customer on this line — that is open.

Everything you asked for, in order.

SPECIFICATION AND WHAT IS IN THE BOX
21": ceramic body 54 cm / 21", 5–8 people, SS304 cooking grid 46.5 cm, assembled 133.5 x 69.5 x 123 cm.
18": ceramic body 45 cm / 18", 4–5 people, SS304 cooking grid 38.3 cm, assembled 103.5 x 59 x 105 cm.
Both ship as a complete unit: ceramic body, cast-iron top vent, bottom draft door, thermometer, stainless cooking grid, charcoal grate and firebox, rolling cart with castors, and two bamboo side shelves. Heat deflector, pizza stone, chimney and covers are add-ons — worth pricing together if you want a ready-to-cook bundle rather than a bare grill on the shop floor.

PRICES — FOB Shenzhen Yantian, USD, Classic series, standard brown carton. Indicative, not a formal PI. The US$255–316 in my first note was the market range comparable 21" units trade at; below is our own factory price.

18" Classic
 · Complete unit (body + cart + 2 bamboo side shelves): US$150
 · Cart, no side shelves: US$142
 · Body only: US$113
 · 252 pcs per 40HQ · carton 60 x 56 x 69.5 cm · gross ~65 kg

21" Classic
 · Complete unit (body + cart + 2 bamboo side shelves): US$186
 · Cart, no side shelves: US$178
 · Body only: US$137
 · 162 pcs per 40HQ · carton 69 x 64 x 90 cm · gross ~85 kg

MOQ is one 40HQ, and colours can be mixed inside it — you are not forced to commit a container per colour, which is usually what makes a multi-colour range plan impossible to justify. Lead time is about one month per 40HQ.

Those are standard-build opening numbers. There is room in them once we are talking container volumes, so tell me the annual figure you would want to land and I will price against that rather than against one box. And if you tell me the route or forwarder you normally use, I will put the landed cost beside your current Atom price points — I would rather you judge this on what it returns per unit than on the FOB alone.

CERTIFICATION
EN 1860-1 and LFGB (304 cooking grid) attached. FDA and ROHS / REACH are also held.

CATALOGUE
The 2025 book is a large file and is mid-revision, so rather than post you last season's numbers I have put the current 18" and 21" detail in this email. Say the word and I will send the full PDF or a link, or pull the pages for any other size you want to look at.

WARRANTY AND SPARES
5 years under normal use. Every shipment carries spare ceramic fireboxes, fire rings and fixings as standard — ceramic chips in transit and in customers' gardens, and the usual reason a retailer drops a kamado range is that a part cannot be got. Gaskets, grids, deflectors and handles are all catalogue items, so replacements are never a hunt.

OEM AND PACKAGING
Logo on the handle is the normal route for a first run; printed colour cartons, wood crate or pallet on request. Standard brown carton is in the prices above.

SAMPLES
18" RMB 1,200, 21" RMB 2,000, FOB Foshan — roughly US$170 and US$280 at today's rate. Same complete unit as production, not a cut-down demo. It sits above the container price because a one-off carries the single-piece glaze and firing setup; it comes back to you as credit against the first order. Allow about 7–10 days for glazing. Send me the delivery postcode and I will come back within 48 hours with the exact freight to your door, passed through at cost with no mark-up from us.

TRIAL ORDERS BELOW A CONTAINER
You would appoint your own forwarder — they collect from the factory, or we deliver to their China warehouse. Per-unit freight is higher than on a full load, so tell me the quantity you have in mind and we will see what can be done with it. On a full 40HQ the handoff is the same.

UK EXCLUSIVITY
Worth discussing once you have a sample in front of you. The shape of it depends on the volume and term you have in mind, and I would rather agree something you can actually hit than something that reads well and then strains.

Next step: give me the size, the colour and a delivery postcode and I will have samples costed and moving. If it is quicker to talk it through, I am on WhatsApp at +86 137 9461 3243 and can take a call in UK hours.

Best,
Kit Lee
Export — YUEFU BBQ, Foshan, China
WhatsApp: +86 137 9461 3243

2026-09-22 · **发邮件**：冷邮 `#386–#400` **15/15**（hero+onepager）· 英 Contacted 97→**112** · 英 A Lead 19→**4**（仅 Bounce）· **英 Contacted≥100 → 停英新冷邮**。

---

## 2026-09-22 回信户跟进（7 户 · **v2 已按回复率优化** · 实发 3 封：`#285` `#169` `#342`）

> Co-founder 2026-09-22 要求「给所有回信询盘的厂家回复 + 生活化问候」。**冲突已报**：`cofounder.md` 护栏明文 **#41 Martin 暂不催 / #169 Brad 不催**，2026-09-10 决议 **禁催 Brad/Martin/Angela/Alex**；`#285` Notes 标「等回」。Co-founder 选 **只看草稿、不发**（语气「中」＝开头人味 1–2 句，正文照常给数字）。**本轮不改 Stage / Last Contact / 漏斗。**
>
> ### v2 的根因诊断（2026-09-22 · 据 `lessons.md` 自有历史）
>
> **4 条热线索死法相同：全部死在「请你先选」那一步。** `#132` Alex 回 Yes → 我方问 18"/21" → 静默 34 天；`#147` Angela 要图 → 我方给图 + 问要不要 one-pager → 静默 39 天；`#285` Paul 要报价 → 我方给价 + 问颜色 → 静默 6 天；`#169` Brad → 我方 9/02 给 YES/LATER/PASS 一词可回 → **连三选一都没回**。`lessons.md` 9/09「热线索在『等对方』上停住」的根因不是跟得不勤，是**每封信结尾都要买家先做决定**。Brad 的反例证明「低摩擦 CTA」本身不是解药——他不知道到门总价，YES 等于对未知金额点头。
>
> **v2 五条改法（逐封执行）：**
> 1. **反转谁先动**：我方替他配好单（默认尺寸+颜色+数量），他只需回一个词确认或纠正；纠正比拟单便宜。
> 2. **给到门总价**，不再「发我邮编我再算」。`#369` 邮编 **PE2 6FT** 已在 `pipeline`、`#169` Western Sydney 已在 Notes、`#285` Thurcroft 已在 Notes —— **不得再问我们已有的信息**。
> 3. **删掉所有 no rush / no pressure 句**：等于书面授权对方不回信。
> 4. **压长度**：单封目标 180–230 词；只留与该户直接相关的数字，不铺全表。
> 5. **真实时间逻辑**：厂 2 月春节停工 → 春季上架是 11 月的决定。**非虚构紧迫感**。
>
> **共用新料（厂 2026-09-21 报价表，此前只发过 `#369`）：** FOB **盐田 USD** · 18" 整机 150 / 有车架无侧台 142 / 仅炉体 113 · 252 pcs/40HQ · 箱 60×56×69.5（**0.23 CBM**）· GW ~65kg；21" 整机 186 / 178 / 仅炉体 137 · 162 pcs/40HQ · 箱 69×64×90（**0.40 CBM**）· GW ~85kg · MOQ = 1×40HQ 可混色 · 交期约 1 月 · 保修正常使用 **5 年** + 随货备件 · 样品 18" RMB1,200 / 21" RMB2,000 FOB 佛山（大货同款整机、可抵货款、运费据实）· 柜下试单客户自找货代。CBM 由箱规算得。
>
> ### 运费估算（2026-09-22 市场行情查证 · **estimate 非报价**）
>
> Co-founder 2026-09-22 授权「Agent 上网查区间，明标 estimate，待正式报价确认」。**来源：** Freightos（深圳→Felixstowe 1 CBM US$507–675 / 49–66 天）· Dantful 2026 LCL 指南（1–2 CBM door-to-door DDU **US$220–500**，含提货/拼柜/报关/末端派送，不含英关税与 VAT）· Zbao（1–3 CBM DDP US$120–180/CBM · **最低计费 1 CBM**）· tonlexing（LCL US$45–120/CBM 纯海运 · 空运 US$4–10.5/kg）· Ubest / docshipper / UpFreights（深圳→悉尼 LCL **US$50–150/CBM** · DDP 海运 US$100–350/CBM · 空运 US$4–7/kg）。
>
> | 线路 | 海运到门（不含税费） | 时效 | 空运到门 | 时效 |
> |---|---|---|---|---|
> | 佛山 → 英国（S66 / PE2） | **US$300–550** / 计费 1 CBM | 6–8 周 | **US$450–700**（85kg 计费） | 1–2 周 |
> | 佛山 → 悉尼 Western Sydney | **US$150–350** / 计费 1 CBM | 3–4 周 | **US$260–455**（65kg 计费） | 约 1 周 |
>
> **⚠️ 最重要的一条（已写进 4 封草稿）：LCL 最低计费 1 CBM，而 21" 单台仅 0.40 CBM、18" 仅 0.23 CBM。** 故：2×21"＝0.80 CBM、1×21"+1×18"＝0.63 CBM、2×18"＝0.46 CBM —— **全部仍在一个计费 CBM 内，第二台几乎不增加运费**（重量 170kg 远低于 1 吨 W/M 分界，体积计费）。这把「加一台」从推销变成算术，是本轮最强的成单杠杆。
>
> **口径要求：** 信内必须写成 *estimate / from current market rates*，并说明厂货代出正式价、**据实转收不加价**；英国明说不含 duty & VAT（HS 仍按 7321.19 假设，**不得报税率**）；澳洲可写 ChAFTA 免关税（`lessons.md` 2026-07-10 已确认），GST/清关归买家。
>
> ### 发信前仍未解决的一个输入
>
> **佣金率（仅 `#342`）** —— `lessons.md` 2026-09-19 明文「佣金比例未谈妥前不编造」。草稿故此**不写任何百分比**，改为请对方报他那行的常规水平 + 我方明说需回厂确认。
>
> **其他禁止：** 21" EN1860-1 未到手前不得声称附上（`#369` 以证书到手为前置）；不写锁价/正式 PI；不点名 Lidl/Aldi；不重发整本目录；样品是大货同款整机，不谎称厂可单做仅炉体样品；春节停工窗口按「2 月」泛写，**精确停工日期需厂确认后才可写**。

### #285 Vision — 跟进 Paul（**v2** · 替他配好样品单 · **已发 2026-09-22** msg `1a0c909d565731c1`）

> 静默 6 天（我方 2026-09-16 已回样品价 + 问颜色 → 无回）。**v2 改法**：不再问颜色，直接默认 **2×21"（黑+灰）**并报到门总价；颜色改由他「纠正」而非「选择」。默认 2 台的理由写成**运费算术**（0.80 CBM 仍在最低计费 1 CBM 内），不是推销；他 9/15 原话就是 "1–2 samples"，等于替他把 2 台这个选项算清。兑现上次承诺的 body-only 数字。地址用 Notes 已有的 Thurcroft，不问邮编。运费已填 estimate（US$300–550 海 / 450–700 空）。附件：无。

**To:** paul@gboutdoorliving.co.uk  
**Cc:** sales@visionoutdoorkitchens.co.uk  
**Subject:** Re: Kamado sourcing for Vision — your 21" sample, costed to Thurcroft

Hi Paul,

Hope the back end of the season treated you well. From here the enquiries seem to have turned from "can you fit it this summer" into "we're planning for spring", which is why I'd rather sort this now than in January.

I left you with a question about colour, which was the wrong way round — you asked me for costs. So let me make the call and you can overrule it.

Unless you say otherwise I'll send **two 21" complete units, one black and one grey**: RMB 2,000 each FOB Foshan, about US$280 a unit, same build as production, both credited back against a first order.

Two rather than one is freight arithmetic, not salesmanship. Sea freight bills a minimum of one cubic metre and a boxed 21" is 0.40 CBM, so the pair still sits inside the same billable cube. Door to door at Thurcroft is around **US$300–550** either way, on 6–8 weeks; air is **US$450–700** in one to two weeks if a showroom date is driving it. Both are estimates from published rates, not quotes — the plant's forwarder confirms it exactly and we pass it through at cost, with duty and VAT outside that. So roughly **US$860–1,110** for the pair delivered against US$580–830 for one. The second grill is mostly just the grill.

I also owed you the body-only option: 21" body alone US$137, with cart but no side shelves US$178, complete unit US$186, FOB Yantian, 162 to a 40HQ with colours mixed inside one container. Five years warranty and spare fireboxes in every shipment — an unsourceable cracked firebox is what usually turns a built-in kamado into a callout you can't close.

Reply "both" and it moves this week, "one" for the black alone, or name a different colour and I'll switch it. If bodies without carts suit your fit-outs better, say "bodies".

One date worth knowing: the plant closes for Chinese New Year in February, so spring floor stock is a November decision rather than a February one.

Best,
Kit Lee
Export — YUEFU BBQ, Foshan, China
WhatsApp: +86 137 9461 3243

### #342 Inferno — 跟进（**v2** · 我方先提结构 · **已发 2026-09-22** msg `1a0c90b229030f8f`）

> 静默 3 天（我方 2026-09-19 问了渠道/尺寸/开票 3 问 → 无回）。**v2 改法**：不催那 3 问，我方先把**佣金结构**摆出来（谁开票 / 谁承担库存风险 / 谁承担成本），让他只需回一个词选路线。**佣金率一律不写**（`lessons.md` 2026-09-19 禁编造），改为请他报常规水平 + 明说需回厂确认。**节奏提示：** 3 天偏早，建议排在 `#285` 之后再发。

**To:** info@infernowoodfiredovens.com  
**Cc:** technical@infernoproducts.com  
**Subject:** Re: Complementary ceramic kamado for Inferno — how the commission side could work

Hi there,

Hope the week's treating you well — late September seems to be when wood-fired enquiries stop being "this weekend" and start being next spring's installs.

I sent you three questions last week and I suspect the honest answer is that they depend on numbers you don't have yet. So let me go first.

The version that costs you nothing: you introduce, we supply and invoice the end customer directly, and you take a percentage of the FOB value on anything that ships. No stock, no landed-cost risk, no outlay if nothing sells. I'm deliberately not inventing a rate — I'd rather you tell me what's normal in your trade and I'll take it back to the plant and confirm it properly than quote you something I have to walk back later.

The alternative, if you'd rather own the customer relationship: you buy at trade and invoice them yourself. 21" complete unit is US$186 FOB Yantian, 18" is US$150, bodies alone US$137 and US$113. Comparable UK ceramic kamados sit around US$255–316 at the supply end, so there's real room in it. MOQ is one 40HQ with colours mixed inside, about a month's lead time, 5 years warranty, spares in every shipment. EN 1860-1, LFGB, FDA and ROHS/REACH are all held.

Reply "commission" or "trade" and I'll come back with the one page that fits. If it's neither, "pass" is a genuinely useful answer.

Best,
Kit Lee

> v5 写法（Co-founder 10/09 改 · 口吻去生硬 · 建议发送顺序 #369 → #285 → #298 → #357 → #342）：像老销售写信 —— 先接上次对方问的事、坦然承认沉默；**请对方直说报价哪里不合适**（价格/规格/时机），表明想帮他的品牌把产品线做成、补齐供应链；数字一段带过（样品 21" US$280 + 海运约 US$350 · 整柜落地 21" 约 US$220 / 18" 约 US$175 · 均可谈 · 样品费抵首单）；结尾一个**容易说「不」的问题**（no-oriented question），降低回信门槛。署名 Kit。
> 落地成本估算（v3 · 待货代确认）：40HQ 盐田→Felixstowe 按 **US$4,000/柜**（公开货代报价 2026-08 盐田 US$4,000–4,450；9 月南沙 US$3,250）+ 英港口/清关/拖车约 **US$1,000/柜** + 关税约 **2%**（UKGT 7321.19，见 `country-scoring.md`）· VAT 20% 不计（注册进口商可抵扣）。21"：186 + 4,000/162 + 1,000/162 + 2% ≈ **US$220/台**；18"：150 + 4,000/252 + 1,000/252 + 2% ≈ **US$175/台**（单一尺寸整柜）。
> **正文不提旧邮箱**（不提 claire、不提换邮箱、不提垃圾箱）。From `leewingqi16@gmail.com`（`user-gmail`），开新线程。

### #285 Vision Outdoor Kitchens — 回访（品牌角度钩子 + 样品报价）

**To:** paul@gboutdoorliving.co.uk  
**Cc:** sales@visionoutdoorkitchens.co.uk  
**Subject:** Your 21" samples

Hi Paul,

When we last spoke you were weighing up one or two 21" samples. I sent a lot of numbers back, and I suspect they raised more questions than they answered.

So let me ask it straight: was there something that didn't work for Vision — the price, the spec, or just the timing? If it's price, tell me where it needs to be. We'd like to be the factory that makes your kitchen range easier to build, and I'd rather shape the offer around that than keep guessing.

For reference, nothing has gone up:
- 21" complete sample: US$280, plus roughly US$350 sea freight to you (I'll firm that up before anything ships)
- Full container: about US$220 per unit landed in the UK, before VAT

Both are negotiable, and the sample cost comes off your first order.

I've attached a few real shots from the showroom, including inside the firebowl, so you can judge the build.

Is now simply the wrong time to be looking at this?

Best,

> 往来核查（2026-10-09 两邮箱）：我方共发 **6** 封（leewingqi16：9/20 冷邮 · 9/21 逐条长回含 FOB+样品价 RMB 1,200/2,000 · 9/22 证书 · 9/22 目录；claire：9/23 换邮箱通知 · 9/24 中秋）；Luke 回 **1** 封（9/20）。9/21 我方要他给邮编再报运费，他未给。

**To:** Luke@atomgrills.co.uk  
**Cc:** sales@atomgrills.co.uk  
**Subject:** Rough landed cost for Atom

Hi Luke,

I sent you a lot in September — prices, certificates, the catalogue — probably more than anyone wants to read in one week. One thing I offered but never did was put a landed cost next to your own price points, so here it is in short.

On a full container, before VAT: about US$220 landed for a complete 21", and about US$175 for an 18". For the sample, rather than wait on a postcode, sea freight to most of England is roughly US$350 on top of the US$280 I quoted. All of it is negotiable, and the sample still comes off your first order.

If Atom is going to put its name on a kamado, it has to fit your range and leave you a proper margin. So if anything in what I sent put you off — the price, the MOQ, the exclusivity point — just say. I'd rather build the offer around what Atom needs than defend a number.

I've attached some real showroom shots, including the firebowl and cast-iron grate.

Have you parked the kamado idea for now?

- one 21" sample: US$280, plus about US$350 sea freight
